=TEXTAFTER(#DIV/0!,"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER(#N/A,"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER("abc","-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER("","-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER(TRUE,"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER(-1,"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER(0,"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER(1.5,"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER(99999999999,"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER(Data!B4,"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER(Data!C1:C2,"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER(Data!A1:B2,"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER({"a","b"},"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER(Data!B11,"-",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER("a-b-c",#DIV/0!,1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER("a-b-c",#N/A,1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER("a-b-c","abc",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER("a-b-c","",1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER("a-b-c",TRUE,1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER("a-b-c",-1,1,0,0,"nf")
=TEXTAFTER("a-b-c",0,1,0,0,"nf")
